Structural cue: spring, cups, and arranged cushions cluster around provision. Role: the row may be read as a gathering around water rather than random seating. Abductive move: water creates social arrangement in a garden. Reading change: the ayah joins comfort to communal access.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000871/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0551d477baf599d633f3","text":"1. **Rows convert comfort into coordinated communal capacity.** **Lexical:** `ص ف ف` B001, \"standing or arranging in a straight row.\" **Cue:** cushions follow a running spring, plural couches, and plural cups, then precede broadly spread carpets. **Role:** the row branch allocates repeated places in a stable relation to one another. **Abduction:** I infer multiple anticipated occupants from plural objects and alignment; no gathering is explicitly narrated. **Reading change:** `مصفوفة` makes comfort social and scalable, not an isolated seat for one person.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000697/B011","root_000871/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3c830534041a6ce35bc2","text":"1. **Cushions ordered in rows as social rest.** Evidence: `ص ف ف` B001 arranging in straight row; `س ر ر` B011 support in 88:13. Structural cue: couches, cups, cushions, carpets unfold as a furnishing sequence. Role: rows give the resting body ordered support. Abductive move: comfort is not chaotic abundance; it is arranged. Reading change: the ayah reads as spatial order serving repose.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7d2f5de1424779cefb76","text":"The later command to look at created order made the rows part of the surah’s larger arrangement logic: camels, sky, mountains, and earth are ordered, and so is the garden interior. The water-gathering branch became visible only after reading spring and cups together.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000871/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_97a4d1564afa78d80514","text":"3. **A small level line anticipates the earth's broad level surface.** **Lexical:** `ص ف ف` B005, \"level smooth ground,\" activated through the row's evenness. **Cue:** 88:16 spreads carpets and 88:20 asks how the earth was made an extended surface. **Role:** the cushion row supplies a domestic line of levelness; the later earth supplies its world-scale plane. **Abduction:** I infer a scale analogy from even row, spread textile, and leveled earth. **Reading change:** the arranged cushions become a miniature habitable surface within the same design logic later displayed by the earth.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000871/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b21aea649e13b8b8f642","text":"2. **Battle-line transformed into hospitality-line.** Evidence: `ص ف ف` B001 includes battle line and arranged groups; `ن ص ب` B008 hostility/war in the earlier branch inventory; `ل غ و` B002 excluded social harm. Structural cue: punitive section contains strain/hostility; garden section arranges rows without conflict. Role: `مصفوفة` preserves the row-image but removes violence. Abductive move: the same social geometry can be hostile or hospitable depending on the governing order. Reading change: the cushions are a pacified formation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000871/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b392206f19a4771dda85","text":"88:16 immediately corrected any reading of the row as exhaustive order: aligned cushions and scattered carpets are complementary distributions. 88:20 supplied the larger retrospective activation, making `ص ف ف` B005 level ground visible as the small-scale precursor of an extended habitable surface.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000871/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d45b9c4424f0c07943a9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:15:2\",\"root_000871\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sıralanmanın cushions. **Role:** B007 assigns the row a provisioning center: bodies can gather without competing disorderly around a source. **Abduction:** I infer spatial relation to the spring or cups from
